- **arsdlc-delta**: Any change of intent after the spec is signed - new requirement, changed behaviour, removed scope, or a bug that reveals the spec was wrong. Writes a delta file, updates the spec explicitly, and invalidates stale approvals on purpose. ([AnshRoshan/aisdlc](https://github.com/AnshRoshan/aisdlc/tree/HEAD/skills/arsdlc-delta))
- **arsdlc-discover**: Double-Diamond discovery intake for a new idea in the standard or regulated lane. Use when the idea is vague, the user says "I want to build" or "new feature", or `arsdlc next` reports DISCOVER. Runs JTBD, workaround mining, 5 Whys, How-Might-We, MoSCoW and the riskiest assumption with the arsdlc-grill interview discipline, seeds docs/glossary.md, writes docs/brief.md and seed EARS requirements. Ends in a verdict - build, spike first, defer, or do not build - so a bad idea dies cheaply. ([AnshRoshan/aisdlc](https://github.com/AnshRoshan/aisdlc/tree/HEAD/skills/arsdlc-discover))
- **arsdlc-flow**: The arsdlc router. Use at the start of ANY engineering task in a repo with an arsdlc.json or docs/features/ directory, when the user says "next", "where are we", "what should I do", "continue", "status", or before any stage work. Reads state from disk via `npx arsdlc next` (or from the files by hand when the CLI is unavailable), respects the feature's lane, reads any handoff, then hands off to exactly one arsdlc-* skill. Never guesses state from chat history. ([AnshRoshan/aisdlc](https://github.com/AnshRoshan/aisdlc/tree/HEAD/skills/arsdlc-flow))
- **arsdlc-grill**: Relentless structured interview that turns a vague plan, idea or spec into shared understanding before anything is built. Use when the user says "grill me", "interview me", "poke holes", "stress-test this", "what am I missing", or when discover, spec, plan, delta or retro needs a human decision. Works the design tree in rounds, gives a recommended answer with every question, finds facts itself instead of asking, writes settled decisions to disk. ([AnshRoshan/aisdlc](https://github.com/AnshRoshan/aisdlc/tree/HEAD/skills/arsdlc-grill))
- **arsdlc-handoff**: Compact the session into a handoff document so a fresh session, another agent, or a human continues without loss. Use when the user says "handoff", "save progress", "I need to stop", "context is getting long", "continue this tomorrow", before /compact or /clear, when the window is over ~70% full, or at the end of a stage that took more than one session. - **art-all**: The one command. Use when the user wants to write, plan, repurpose, audit or publish for Medium or AWS Builder Center but does not know or does not name which specific skill to use.
